  > Thick skin is the part most people underestimate — it hides the definition for months, so the six-week photo can look nothing like the nose at a year. As an Iranian guy with thick skin heading to Turkey, the honest check is usually whether a surgeon shows healed results on thick-skinned men, and whether they talk about harmony rather than just a smaller nose. When you weigh up the names people suggest, do you put more weight on their long-term photos on thick skin, or on how they explain what the skin can and can't do?
